Title: Origenis in Epistolam ad Romanos Commentariorum libri (Rufinus's Latin), Origenis Opera Omnia, Tomus VII: Books VI to X of the Commentary on the Epistle to the Romans
Creator: Origen (Rufinus of Aquileia, translator and abridger, c. 406-410); edited by Carolus and Carol. Vincent. De la Rue (Maurists), newly revised by Carol. Henric. Eduard. Lommatzsch
Publisher: Berolini (Berlin): Sumtibus Haude et Spener, 1837
Publication Date: 1837
Rights: Public Domain
Language: lat (Rufinus's Latin; Lommatzsch's notes and variant readings are Latin, with some Greek)
Source: Internet Archive item origenous-ta-heuriskomena-panta-t.-7-1837; the item's own OCR text file (djvu.txt), fetched 2026-10-26 by the Library thread.
Provenance note: The item's metadata names the work, the 1837 Berlin imprint and a Public Domain Mark 1.0 licence (licenseurl creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/); it was published in 1837, so it is out of US copyright by date as well. The text below is the host's OCR, unchanged. Only this header was added. The title page was read in the OCR and names the volume and the editors above.
Content note: Books VI-X, followed by the volume's indexes. Books I-V are volume VI. The Latin is Rufinus's translation. In his preface (npnf203) he says the Greek commentary ran to fifteen volumes and that he was asked to abbreviate it; this Latin has ten books. Jerome accused him of altering Origen (that quarrel is also in npnf203). It is evidence for Origen as Rufinus gave him, not Origen verbatim. The OCR is readable Latin with occasional misreadings and stray superscript marks from the footnote apparatus. Check any quotation against the page image before it goes into a record.

. regno coelorum praedicando, pro litera legis abjicienda,
